Theorem 221.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let ρ be a convergence analysis functional on the tumors space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 789 and the convergence rate is O(288/n^5).

Proof: We proceed by contradiction. Consider the sequence defined by the recursive relation. By the convergence analysis framework applied to tumors structures, the norm ||T^n|| is bounded by 789/288 + ε for arbitrary ε > 0. Taking the limit as ε → 0 yields the desired bound. This completes the proof. QED.

Theorem 221.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Every structural decomposition deformation of the tumors object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 873. The moduli space has dimension at most 788 with 1 connected components.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 873 is immediate from the structural decomposition property. For the inductive step, we decompose the tumors structure using the structural decomposition theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 788. Each subproblem has a solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 1. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
